theorem AnvBandit.combine_timeorder_bounds : ∀ {β : ℝ},
  1 < β →
    ∀ (R K : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (C0 C1 CK : ℝ),
      0 ≤ C0 →
        0 ≤ C1 →
          0 ≤ CK →
            (∀ (T H : ℕ), 1 ≤ T → 1 ≤ H → R T H ≤ C0 + C1 * K T H + C1 * Real.log (↑T + 1)) →
              (∀ (T H : ℕ),
                  1 ≤ T → 1 ≤ H → K T H ≤ CK * (↑H + Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ (β / (β - 1)) / ↑H ^ (β / (β - 1) - 1))) →
                AnvBandit.TimeOrder β R :=
fun {β} _hβ R K C0 C1 CK h0 h1 hK hbudget hKbd =>
  Exists.intro (C0 + C1 * CK + C1)
    ⟨add_nonneg (add_nonneg h0 (mul_nonneg h1 hK)) h1, fun T H hT hH =>
      have hlogT :=
        Real.log_nonneg
          (cast
            (Eq.symm
              (Eq.trans (congr (congrArg LE.le (Eq.symm Nat.cast_one)) (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd ↑T) (Eq.symm Nat.cast_one)))
                (Eq.trans (congrArg (LE.le ↑1) (Nat.cast_add._simp_1 T 1)) Nat.cast_le._simp_1)))
            (have this := Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.combine_timeorder_bounds._proof_1_1 T H hT a;
            this));
      have hQ0 :=
        div_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg hlogT (β / (β - 1)))
          (Real.rpow_nonneg
            (le_of_lt
              (Nat.cast_pos'.mpr
                (lt_of_lt_of_le
                  (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℕ (Eq.refl 1))
                    (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 1)))
                  hH)))
            (β / (β - 1) - 1));
      have h1' := hbudget T H hT hH;
      have h2' := hKbd T H hT hH;
      have hbnd :=
        le_of_not_gt fun a =>
          Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
            (Eq.mp
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (↑H) rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg (fun _a => ↑H ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ↑H ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (↑H ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (↑H) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                    (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                              Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                              (↑H ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.log (↑T + 1)) rfl
                            (Eq.mpr

def AnvBandit.DecayModel.HKappa : {n K : ℕ} → AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → ℝ → ℕ :=
fun {n K} M L => (Finset.powersetCard (AnvBandit.kappa n K) M.others).sup' ⋯ fun F => ∑ k ∈ F, M.mkAll L k
def AnvBandit.DecayModel.ofPositiveCurves : {n K : ℕ} →
  (b : Fin K → ℝ) →
    (f : Fin K → ℕ → ℝ) →
      (star : Fin K) →
        (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 < b k) →
          (∀ (k : Fin K), b k ≤ 1) →
            (∀ (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ), 1 ≤ s → 0 ≤ f k s) →
              (∀ (k : Fin K) (s t : ℕ), 1 ≤ s → s ≤ t → f k t ≤ f k s) →
                (∀ (k : Fin K), Filter.Tendsto (f k) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) →
                  (∀ (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ), 1 ≤ s → f k s ≤ 1 - b k) →
                    (∀ (k : Fin K), b k ≤ b star) →
                      (∀ (k : Fin K), b k = b star → k = star) → 2 ≤ n → 2 ≤ K → AnvBandit.DecayModel n K :=
fun {n K} b f star b_pos b_le_one f_nonneg f_anti f_tendsto f_le_gap b_le_star star_unique two_le_n two_le_K =>
  { b := b, f := fun k => AnvBandit.extendPositiveCurve (f k), star := star, b_pos := b_pos, b_le_one := b_le_one,
    f_nonneg := ⋯, f_anti := ⋯, f_tendsto := ⋯, f_le_gap := ⋯, b_le_star := b_le_star, star_unique := star_unique,
    two_le_n := two_le_n, two_le_K := two_le_K }

def AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible : {n : ℕ} →
  0 < n →
    (P : Fin n → Fin n → ℝ) →
      (∀ (i j : Fin n), 0 ≤ P i j) →
        (∀ (i : Fin n), ∑ j, P i j = 1) →
          (∀ (a b : Fin n), Relation.ReflTransGen (fun x y => 0 < P y x) a b) → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n :=
fun {n} hn P hP hrow hirr =>
  { P := P, nonneg := hP, row_sum := hrow,
    pmin := (AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.positiveEntries P).inf' ⋯ fun ij => P ij.1 ij.2, pmin_pos := ⋯, le_pmin := ⋯,
    irreducible := hirr }
'AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
theorem AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible_pmin_attained : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (P : Fin n → Fin n → ℝ)
  (hP : ∀ (i j : Fin n), 0 ≤ P i j) (hrow : ∀ (i : Fin n), ∑ j, P i j = 1)
  (hirr : ∀ (a b : Fin n), Relation.ReflTransGen (fun x y => 0 < P y x) a b),
  ∃ i j, 0 < P i j ∧ (AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible hn P hP hrow hirr).pmin = P i j :=
fun {n} hn P hP hrow hirr =>
  Exists.casesOn
    (Finset.exists_mem_eq_inf' (AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.positiveEntries_nonempty hn P hrow) fun ij => P ij.1 ij.2)
    fun ij h =>
    And.casesOn h fun hij heq => Exists.intro ij.1 (Exists.intro ij.2 ⟨(Finset.mem_filter.mp hij).right, heq⟩)
'AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible_pmin_attained' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
theorem AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.measure_good_compl_le_finite.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} [inst : MeasurableSpace Ω]
  {n K T : ℕ} (μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (F : MeasureTheory.Filtration ℕ inst)
  (selected : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → Ω → Bool) (X p : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → Ω → ℝ),
  2 ≤ n →
    2 ≤ K →
      1 ≤ T →
        (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K), ∀ t < T, MeasurableSet {ω | selected i k t ω = true}) →
          (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K), ∀ t < T, MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (X i k t)) →
            (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K), ∀ t < T, MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (p i k t)) →
              (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K), ∀ t < T, ∀ (ω : Ω), X i k t ω = 0 ∨ X i k t ω = 1) →
                (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K), ∀ t < T, ∀ (ω : Ω), p i k t ω ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) →
                  (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K), ∀ t < T, μ[X i k t | ↑F t] =ᵐ[μ] p i k t) →
                    ∀ (δ : ℝ),
                      0 < δ →
                        δ ≤ 1 →
                          μ.real (AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.good selected X p T (Real.log (↑(2 * n * K * T) / δ)))ᶜ ≤
                            δ :=
fun {Ω} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {n K T} μ [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] F selected X p hn hK hT hs hXm hpm hX01
    hp01 hmean δ hδ hδ1 =>
  let s' := fun i k t ω => if t < T then selected i k t ω else false;
  let X' := fun i k t ω => if t < T then X i k t ω else 0;
  let p' := fun i k t ω => if t < T then p i k t ω else 0;
  have hs' := fun i k t =>
    if ht : t < T then
      Eq.mpr
        (id
          (Eq.trans
            (congrArg MeasurableSet
              (congrArg setOf
                (funext fun ω =>
                  congrFun'
                    (congrArg Eq
                      (Eq.trans
                        (congrFun'
                          (congrFun'
                            (congrFun'
                              (congrFun'
                                (funext fun i =>
                                  funext fun k =>
                                    funext fun t => funext fun ω => Bool.if_false_right (t < T) (selected i k t ω))
                                i)
                              k)
                            t)
                          ω)
                        (Eq.trans
                          (congrFun'
                            (congrArg and
                              (Eq.trans (Decidable.decide.congr_simp (t < T) True (eq_true ht))
                                (decide_true instDecidableTrue)))
                            (selected i k t ω))
                          (Bool.true_and (selected i k t ω)))))
                    true)))
            measurableSet_setOf._simp_1))
        (Eq.mp measurableSet_setOf._simp_1 (hs i k t ht))
    else
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrArg MeasurableSet
            (congrArg setOf
              (funext fun ω =>
                Eq.trans
                  (congrFun'
                    (congrArg Eq
                      (Eq.trans
                        (congrFun'
                          (congrFun'
                            (congrFun'
                              (congrFun'
                                (funext fun i =>
                                  funext fun k =>
                                    funext fun t => funext fun ω => Bool.if_false_right (t < T) (selected i k t ω))
                                i)
                              k)
                            t)
                          ω)
                        (Eq.trans
                          (congrFun'
                            (congrArg and
                              (Eq.trans (Decidable.decide.congr_simp (t < T) False (eq_false ht))
                                (decide_false instDecidableFalse)))
                            (selected i k t ω))
                          (Bool.false_and (selected i k t ω)))))
                    true)
                  Bool.false_eq_true)))
          MeasurableSet.empty._simp_1);
  have hX' := fun i k t =>
    if ht : t < T then
      Eq.mpr
        (id (congrArg MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (funext fun ω => ite_cond_eq_true (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht))))
        (hXm i k t ht)
    else
      Eq.mpr
        (id (congrArg MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (funext fun ω => ite_cond_eq_false (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_false ht))))
        MeasureTheory.stronglyMeasurable_const;
  have hp' := fun i k t =>
    if ht : t < T then
      Eq.mpr
        (id (congrArg MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (funext fun ω => ite_cond_eq_true (p i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht))))
        (hpm i k t ht)
    else
      Eq.mpr
        (id (congrArg MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable (funext fun ω => ite_cond_eq_false (p i k t ω) 0 (eq_false ht))))
        MeasureTheory.stronglyMeasurable_const;
  have hX01' := fun i k t ω =>
    if ht : t < T then
      Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congr (congrArg Or (congrFun' (congrArg Eq (ite_cond_eq_true (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht))) 0))
            (congrFun' (congrArg Eq (ite_cond_eq_true (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht))) 1)))
        (hX01 i k t ht ω)
    else
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congr
            (congrArg Or
              (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg Eq (ite_cond_eq_false (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_false ht))) 0) (eq_self 0)))
            (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg Eq (ite_cond_eq_false (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_false ht))) 1) zero_ne_one._simp_1))
          (or_false True));
  have hp01' := fun i k t ω =>
    if ht : t < T then
      Eq.mpr
        (id
          (Eq.trans (congrArg (Membership.mem (Set.Icc 0 1)) (ite_cond_eq_true (p i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht)))
            Set.mem_Icc._simp_1))
        (Eq.mp Set.mem_Icc._simp_1 (hp01 i k t ht ω))
    else
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (Eq.trans (congrArg (Membership.mem (Set.Icc 0 1)) (ite_cond_eq_false (p i k t ω) 0 (eq_false ht)))
            Set.mem_Icc._simp_1)
          (Eq.trans (congr (congrArg And (Std.le_refl._simp_1 0)) zero_le_one._simp_1) (and_self True)));
  have hmean' := fun i k t =>
    if ht : t < T then
      Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congr
            (congrArg (MeasureTheory.ae μ).EventuallyEq
              (congrArg (MeasureTheory.condExp (↑F t) μ) (funext fun ω => ite_cond_eq_true (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht))))
            (funext fun ω => ite_cond_eq_true (p i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht))))
        (hmean i k t ht)
    else
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congr
            (congrArg (MeasureTheory.ae μ).EventuallyEq
              (Eq.trans
                (congrArg (MeasureTheory.condExp (↑F t) μ)
                  (funext fun ω => ite_cond_eq_false (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_false ht)))
                MeasureTheory.condExp_fun_zero))
            (funext fun ω => ite_cond_eq_false (p i k t ω) 0 (eq_false ht)))
          (Filter.EventuallyEq.refl._simp_1 (MeasureTheory.ae μ) fun x => 0));
  have heq := fun L =>
    Set.ext fun ω =>
      have hc := fun i k =>
        AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.count_congr_prefix fun t ht =>
          of_eq_true
            (Eq.trans
              (congrFun'
                (congrArg Eq
                  (Eq.trans
                    (congrFun'
                      (congrFun'
                        (congrFun'
                          (congrFun'
                            (funext fun i =>
                              funext fun k =>
                                funext fun t => funext fun ω => Bool.if_false_right (t < T) (selected i k t ω))
                            i)
                          k)
                        t)
                      ω)
                    (Eq.trans
                      (congrFun'
                        (congrArg and
                          (Eq.trans (Decidable.decide.congr_simp (t < T) True (eq_true ht))
                            (decide_true instDecidableTrue)))
                        (selected i k t ω))
                      (Bool.true_and (selected i k t ω)))))
                (selected i k t ω))
              (eq_self (selected i k t ω)));
      have hz := fun i k m =>
        AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.centered_congr_prefix
          (fun t ht =>
            of_eq_true
              (Eq.trans
                (congrFun'
                  (congrArg Eq
                    (Eq.trans
                      (congrFun'
                        (congrFun'
                          (congrFun'
                            (congrFun'
                              (funext fun i =>
                                funext fun k =>
                                  funext fun t => funext fun ω => Bool.if_false_right (t < T) (selected i k t ω))
                              i)
                            k)
                          t)
                        ω)
                      (Eq.trans
                        (congrFun'
                          (congrArg and
                            (Eq.trans (Decidable.decide.congr_simp (t < T) True (eq_true ht))
                              (decide_true instDecidableTrue)))
                          (selected i k t ω))
                        (Bool.true_and (selected i k t ω)))))
                  (selected i k t ω))
                (eq_self (selected i k t ω))))
          (fun t ht =>
            of_eq_true
              (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg Eq (ite_cond_eq_true (X i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht))) (X i k t ω))
                (eq_self (X i k t ω))))
          fun t ht =>
          of_eq_true
            (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg Eq (ite_cond_eq_true (p i k t ω) 0 (eq_true ht))) (p i k t ω))
              (eq_self (p i k t ω)));
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrFun'
            (congrArg Iff
              (forall_congr fun i =>
                forall_congr fun k =>
                  forall_congr fun m =>
                    implies_congr (Eq.refl (1 ≤ m))
                      (implies_congr (congrArg (LE.le m) ((fun i k => hc i k) i k))
                        (congrFun' (congrArg LE.le (congrArg abs ((fun i k m => hz i k m) i k m)))
                          (↑m * AnvBandit.rdelta L m)))))
            (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (m : ℕ),
              1 ≤ m →
                m ≤ AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.count (selected i k) T ω →
                  |AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.centered (selected i k) (X i k) (p i k) m T ω| ≤ ↑m * AnvBandit.rdelta L m))
          (iff_self
            (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (m : ℕ),
              1 ≤ m →
                m ≤ AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.count (selected i k) T ω →
                  |AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.centered (selected i k) (X i k) (p i k) m T ω| ≤
                    ↑m * AnvBandit.rdelta L m)));
  have hb :=
    AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.measure_good_compl_le μ F s' X' p' hn hK hT hs' hX' hp' hX01' hp01' hmean' δ hδ hδ1;
  Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => μ.real _aᶜ ≤ δ) (heq (Real.log (↑(2 * n * K * T) / δ)))) hb
'AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.measure_good_compl_le_finite' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
structure AnvBandit.Execution.Params (n K : ℕ) : Type
number of parameters: 2
fields:
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.hn : 2 ≤ n
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.hK : 2 ≤ K
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.T : ℕ
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.hT : 1 ≤ self.T
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.h : ℕ
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.hh : 1 ≤ self.h
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.H : ℕ
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.hH : 1 ≤ self.H
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.hdiv : self.h ∣ self.H
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.δ : ℝ
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.hδ : 0 < self.δ ∧ self.δ < 1
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.β : ℝ
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.hβ : 1 < self.β
constructor:
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params.mk {n K : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) (hK : 2 ≤ K) (T : ℕ) (hT : 1 ≤ T) (h : ℕ) (hh : 1 ≤ h) (H : ℕ)
    (hH : 1 ≤ H) (hdiv : h ∣ H) (δ : ℝ) (hδ : 0 < δ ∧ δ < 1) (β : ℝ) (hβ : 1 < β) : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K

    (C : Fin K → ℕ) : AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState n K i
structure AnvBandit.GoodRunOn {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (T : ℕ) : Type
number of parameters: 4
fields:
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.L : ℝ
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.hL : 0 ≤ self.L
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.I : ℕ → Fin n → Fin K
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.bhat : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → ℝ
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.meanSum : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → ℝ
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.mean_bounds : ∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (m : ℕ),
      1 ≤ m →
        m ≤ AnvBandit.pullCount self.I T i k →
          M.b k ≤ self.meanSum i k m / ↑m ∧ self.meanSum i k m / ↑m ≤ M.b k + AnvBandit.psi (M.f k) m
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.hc : ∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (m : ℕ),
      1 ≤ m →
        m ≤ AnvBandit.pullCount self.I T i k → |self.bhat i k m - self.meanSum i k m / ↑m| ≤ AnvBandit.rdelta self.L m
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.active : ℕ → Fin n → Finset (Fin K)
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.sel_mem : ∀ t < T, ∀ (i : Fin n), self.I t i ∈ self.active t i
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.ucb : ∀ t < T,
      ∀ (i : Fin n),
        M.star ∈ self.active t i →
          AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i (self.I t i) = 0 ∨
            1 ≤ AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i (self.I t i) ∧
              1 ≤ AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i M.star ∧
                self.bhat i M.star (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i M.star) +
                    AnvBandit.rdelta self.L (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i M.star) ≤
                  self.bhat i (self.I t i) (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i (self.I t i)) +
                    AnvBandit.rdelta self.L (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i (self.I t i))
constructor:
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.mk {n K : ℕ} {M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K} {T : ℕ} (L : ℝ) (hL : 0 ≤ L) (I : ℕ → Fin n → Fin K)
    (bhat meanSum : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → ℝ)
    (mean_bounds :
      ∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (m : ℕ),
        1 ≤ m →
          m ≤ AnvBandit.pullCount I T i k →
            M.b k ≤ meanSum i k m / ↑m ∧ meanSum i k m / ↑m ≤ M.b k + AnvBandit.psi (M.f k) m)
    (hc :
      ∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (m : ℕ),
        1 ≤ m → m ≤ AnvBandit.pullCount I T i k → |bhat i k m - meanSum i k m / ↑m| ≤ AnvBandit.rdelta L m)
    (active : ℕ → Fin n → Finset (Fin K)) (sel_mem : ∀ t < T, ∀ (i : Fin n), I t i ∈ active t i)
    (ucb :
      ∀ t < T,
        ∀ (i : Fin n),
          M.star ∈ active t i →
            AnvBandit.pullCount I t i (I t i) = 0 ∨
              1 ≤ AnvBandit.pullCount I t i (I t i) ∧
                1 ≤ AnvBandit.pullCount I t i M.star ∧
                  bhat i M.star (AnvBandit.pullCount I t i M.star) +
                      AnvBandit.rdelta L (AnvBandit.pullCount I t i M.star) ≤
                    bhat i (I t i) (AnvBandit.pullCount I t i (I t i)) +
                      AnvBandit.rdelta L (AnvBandit.pullCount I t i (I t i))) :
    AnvBandit.GoodRunOn M T
theorem AnvBandit.Execution.run_congr : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (X X' : ℕ → Fin n → ℝ)
  (J J' : ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) (t : ℕ),
  (∀ s < t, X s = X' s) →
    (∀ (ℓ : ℕ), p.phaseEnd ℓ ≤ t → p.phaseEnd ℓ < p.T → J ℓ = J' ℓ) →
      AnvBandit.Execution.run p X J t = AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t :=
fun {n K} p X X' J J' t hX hJ =>
  Nat.recAux (motive := fun t =>
    (∀ s < t, X s = X' s) →
      (∀ (ℓ : ℕ), p.phaseEnd ℓ ≤ t → p.phaseEnd ℓ < p.T → J ℓ = J' ℓ) →
        AnvBandit.Execution.run p X J t = AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t)
    (fun hX hJ => Eq.refl (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X J 0))
    (fun t ih hX hJ =>
      have he :=
        ih (fun s hs => hX s (Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Execution.run_congr._proof_1_1 t s hs a))
          fun ℓ hℓ hT =>
          hJ ℓ (Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Execution.run_congr._proof_1_2 p t ℓ hℓ a) hT;
      Eq.mpr
        (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' (t + 1)) (AnvBandit.Execution.run_succ p X J t)))
        (Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a =>
                AnvBandit.Execution.step p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X J t) (X t)
                    (J (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X J t).phase) =
                  _a)
              (AnvBandit.Execution.run_succ p X' J' t)))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg
                (fun _a =>
                  AnvBandit.Execution.step p t _a (X t) (J _a.phase) =
                    AnvBandit.Execution.step p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t)
                      (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase))
                he))
            (if ht : t < p.T then
              Eq.mpr
                (id
                  (congr
                    (congrArg Eq
                      (Eq.trans
                        (ite_congr (Eq.refl (t < p.T))
                          (fun a =>
                            ite_congr
                              (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t)))
                              (fun a =>
                                congrFun'
                                  (congrArg AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                                    (congrArg (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                                      (hX t
                                        (Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.Execution.run_congr._proof_1_4 p t a))))
                                  (J (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase))
                              fun a =>
                              congrArg (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                                (hX t
                                  (Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Execution.run_congr._proof_1_4 p t a)))
                          fun a => Eq.refl (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                        ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_pos ht) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3) (t.decLt p.T)
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.State n K)
                          (if AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) then
                            AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                              (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                              (J (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase)
                          else AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))))
                    (Eq.trans
                      (ite_congr (Eq.refl (t < p.T))
                        (fun a =>
                          Eq.refl
                            (if AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) then
                              AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                                (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                                (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase)
                            else AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t)))
                        fun a => Eq.refl (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                      ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_pos ht) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3) (t.decLt p.T)
                        (AnvBandit.Execution.State n K)
                        (if AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) then
                          AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                            (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase)
                        else AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                        (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t)))))
                (if hc : AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) then
                  Eq.mpr
                    (id
                      (congr
                        (congrArg Eq
                          ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_pos hc) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3)
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.instDecidableCommunicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.State n K)
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                              (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                              (J (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase))
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))))
                        ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_pos hc) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3)
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.instDecidableCommunicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.State n K)
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                            (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase))
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t)))))
                    (Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congrArg
                          (fun _a =>
                            AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                                (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t)) _a =
                              AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                                (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                                (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase))
                          (hJ (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase
                            (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ t + 1) (Eq.symm hc.left))) (le_refl (t + 1)))
                            (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a < p.T) (Eq.symm hc.left))) hc.right))))
                      (Eq.refl
                        (AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                          (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase))))
                else
                  of_eq_true
                    (Eq.trans
                      (congr
                        (congrArg Eq
                          ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_neg hc) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3)
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.instDecidableCommunicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.State n K)
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                              (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                              (J (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase))
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))))
                        ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_neg hc) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3)
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.instDecidableCommunicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.State n K)
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                            (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase))
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))))
                      (eq_self (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t)))))
            else
              of_eq_true
                (Eq.trans
                  (congr
                    (congrArg Eq
                      (Eq.trans
                        (ite_congr (Eq.refl (t < p.T))
                          (fun a =>
                            Eq.refl
                              (if AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) then
                                AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                                  (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X t))
                                  (J (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase)
                              else AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X t)))
                          fun a => Eq.refl (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                        ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_neg ht) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3) (t.decLt p.T)
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.State n K)
                          (if AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) then
                            AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                              (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X t))
                              (J (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase)
                          else AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X t))
                          (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))))
                    (Eq.trans
                      (ite_congr (Eq.refl (t < p.T))
                        (fun a =>
                          Eq.refl
                            (if AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) then
                              AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                                (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                                (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase)
                            else AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t)))
                        fun a => Eq.refl (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))
                      ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_neg ht) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3) (t.decLt p.T)
                        (AnvBandit.Execution.State n K)
                        (if AnvBandit.Execution.communicates p t (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) then
                          AnvBandit.Execution.communicate
                            (AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                            (J' (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t).phase)
                        else AnvBandit.Execution.observe p (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t) (X' t))
                        (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t))))
                  (eq_self (AnvBandit.Execution.run p X' J' t)))))))
    t hX hJ
'AnvBandit.Execution.run_congr' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
theorem AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.global_wait_cost : ∀ {n K : ℕ} {M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K} {T : ℕ}
  (R : AnvBandit.GoodRunOn M T),
  0 < n →
    ∀ (c : Fin K → ℝ) (cmax : ℝ) (S : ℕ) (i0 : Fin n) (D : Fin n → Finset (Fin K)),
      (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 ≤ c k) →
        c M.star = 0 →
          (∀ (k : Fin K), c k ≤ cmax) →
            (∀ (t : ℕ) (i : Fin n), S ≤ t → t < T → R.active t i = AnvBandit.permSet n K i ∪ D i) →
              M.star ∈ AnvBandit.permSet n K i0 →
                (∀ (i : Fin n), Disjoint (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) →
                  (∀ (i : Fin n), (D i).card ≤ 2) →
                    (∀ (i : Fin n), M.star ∈ AnvBandit.permSet n K i ∪ D i) →
                      (∀ (i : Fin n), (AnvBandit.permSet n K i ∪ D i).card ≤ AnvBandit.smax n K) →
                        ∑ t ∈ Finset.range T, ∑ i, c (R.I t i) ≤ ↑n * cmax * ↑(min T S) + M.Ccap c R.L :=
fun {n K} {M} {T} R hn c cmax S i0 D hc hstar hcmax hstab hstar_perm hdisj hDcard hstar_mem hSbound =>
  Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ ↑n * cmax * ↑(min T S) + M.Ccap c R.L) (AnvBandit.loss_split_at R.I c T S)))
    (add_le_add (AnvBandit.waiting_cost_le R.I c cmax hcmax T S)
      (AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.terminal_cost R hn c S i0 D hc hstar hstab hstar_perm hdisj hDcard hstar_mem hSbound))
'AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.global_wait_cost' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
def AnvBandit.IsExponential : {n K : ℕ} → AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → (Fin K → ℝ) → (Fin K → ℝ) → Prop :=
fun {n K} M a eta =>
  (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 ≤ a k) ∧
    (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 < eta k) ∧ ∀ (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ), 1 ≤ s → M.f k s = a k * Real.exp (-eta k * (↑s - 1))
theorem AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.proposition_A_4_full : ∀ {n K T : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (a eta : Fin K → ℝ),
  AnvBandit.IsExponential M a eta → AnvBandit.Vstar1 M = AnvBandit.greedyVal n ⋯ M.b a eta T :=
fun {n K T} M a eta hexp =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          _a =
            AnvBandit.greedyVal n
              (have this := M.two_le_K;
              Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.Vstar1_eq_expBench._proof_1 this a)
              M.b a eta T)
        (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.Vstar1_eq_expBench M a eta hexp)))
    (AnvBandit.proposition_A_4 n
      (have this := M.two_le_K;
      Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.Vstar1_eq_expBench._proof_1 this a)
      M.b a eta hexp.left hexp.right.left T)
theorem AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.greedy_policy_attains : ∀ {n K T : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (a eta : Fin K → ℝ),
  AnvBandit.IsExponential M a eta →
    AnvBandit.Vstar1 M =
      AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value M.b a eta 0
        (List.map AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.pure
          (AnvBandit.Greedy.policy ⋯ (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0) T)) :=
fun {n K T} M a eta hexp =>
  let hK :=
    have this := M.two_le_K;
    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.Vstar1_eq_expBench._proof_1 this a;
  let ks := AnvBandit.Greedy.policy hK (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0) T;
  have hlen := AnvBandit.Greedy.policy_length hK (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0) T;
  id
    (le_antisymm
      (id
        (Finset.sup'_le (AnvBandit.Vstar0._proof_2 M) (AnvBandit.J1 M) fun u a_1 =>
          Exists.casesOn (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.joint_le_synchronous M a eta hexp u) fun v hv =>
            have hg :=
              AnvBandit.Greedy.payoff_le_policy hK (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0)
                (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg_antitone M.b a eta 0 hexp.left hexp.right.left) (List.ofFn v);
            Eq.mpr
              (id
                (congrArg (fun _a => AnvBandit.J1 M u ≤ _a)
                  (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value_sync_eq_payoff M.b a eta 0 ks)))
              (LE.le.trans
                (Eq.mp
                  (congrArg (fun _a => AnvBandit.J1 M u ≤ _a)
                    (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value_sync_eq_payoff M.b a eta 0 (List.ofFn v)))
                  (Eq.mp
                    (congrArg (fun _a => AnvBandit.J1 M u ≤ _a)
                      (Eq.symm (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value_sync_ofFn M.b a eta v)))
                    hv))
                (Eq.mp
                  (congrArg
                    (LE.le (AnvBandit.Greedy.payoff (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0) (List.ofFn v)))
                    (congrArg (AnvBandit.Greedy.payoff (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0))
                      (AnvBandit.Greedy.policy.congr_simp hK (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0)
                        (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0)
                        (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.stateMarg M.b a eta 0)) (List.ofFn v).length T
                        List.length_ofFn)))
                  hg))))
      (have hv := AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.exists_ofFn_length ks hlen;
      Exists.casesOn hv fun v hv =>
        Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a =>
                AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value M.b a eta 0 (List.map AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.pure _a) ≤
                  AnvBandit.Vstar1 M)
              (Eq.symm hv)))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ AnvBandit.Vstar1 M) (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value_sync_ofFn M.b a eta v)))
            (have heq :=
              Eq.mpr
                (id
                  (congrArg (fun _a => _a = AnvBandit.syncExpVal n M.b a eta v)
                    (Eq.symm (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value_eq_J1 M a eta hexp fun x t => v t))))
                (have hl :=
                  Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = List.ofFn fun t x => v t) List.map_ofFn))
                    (Eq.refl (List.ofFn (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.pure ∘ v)));
                Eq.mpr
                  (id
                    (congrArg
                      (fun _a => AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value M.b a eta 0 _a = AnvBandit.syncExpVal n M.b a eta v)
                      (Eq.symm hl)))
                  (AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.value_sync_ofFn M.b a eta v));
            Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ AnvBandit.Vstar1 M) (Eq.symm heq)))
              (Finset.le_sup' (AnvBandit.J1 M) (Finset.mem_univ fun x t => v t))))))
'AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.proposition_A_4_full' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.ExponentialDP.greedy_policy_attains' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
theorem AnvBandit.Vstar0_eq_n_Vser : ∀ {n K T : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K),
  AnvBandit.Vstar0 M = ↑n * AnvBandit.Vser M T :=
fun {n K T} M =>
  le_antisymm
    (id
      (Finset.sup'_le (AnvBandit.Vstar0._proof_2 M) (AnvBandit.J0 M) fun u a =>
        Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ ↑n * AnvBandit.Vser M T) (AnvBandit.J0_eq_serial M u)))
          (Trans.trans (Finset.sum_le_sum fun i x => AnvBandit.le_Vser M (AnvBandit.sum_Ntot_eq u i))
            (of_eq_true
              (Eq.trans
                (congrFun'
                  (congrArg Eq
                    (Eq.trans (Finset.sum_const (AnvBandit.Vser M T))
                      (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg HSMul.hSMul (Fintype.card_fin n)) (AnvBandit.Vser M T))
                        (nsmul_eq_mul n (AnvBandit.Vser M T)))))
                  (↑n * AnvBandit.Vser M T))
                (eq_self (↑n * AnvBandit.Vser M T)))))))
    (Exists.casesOn (Finset.exists_mem_eq_sup' (AnvBandit.comps_nonempty M T) fun q => M.serialAlloc fun k => ↑(q k))
      fun q h =>
      And.casesOn h fun hq hmax =>
        have hsum := (Finset.mem_filter.mp hq).right;
        Exists.casesOn (AnvBandit.exists_sched_with_counts (fun k => ↑(q k)) T hsum) fun v hv =>
          have hval :=
            Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = ↑n * AnvBandit.Vser M T) (AnvBandit.J0_eq_serial M fun _i t => v t)))
              (have hcnt := fun i => funext fun k => hv k;
              Eq.mpr
                (id
                  (congrFun'
                    (congrArg Eq
                      (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                        congrArg M.serialAlloc (funext fun k => congrFun ((fun i => hcnt i) x) k)))
                    (↑n * AnvBandit.Vser M T)))
                (Eq.mpr
                  (id
                    (congrFun'
                      (congrArg Eq
                        (Eq.trans (Finset.sum_const (M.serialAlloc fun k => ↑(q k)))
                          (Eq.trans
                            (congrFun' (congrArg HSMul.hSMul (Fintype.card_fin n)) (M.serialAlloc fun k => ↑(q k)))
                            (nsmul_eq_mul n (M.serialAlloc fun k => ↑(q k))))))
                      (↑n * AnvBandit.Vser M T)))
                  ((fun {α β γ} [HMul α β γ] a a_1 e_a =>
                      Eq.rec (motive := fun a_2 e_a => ∀ (a_3 a_4 : β), a_3 = a_4 → a * a_3 = a_2 * a_4)
                        (fun a_2 a_3 e_a => e_a ▸ Eq.refl (a * a_2)) e_a)
                    (↑n) (↑n) (Eq.refl ↑n) (M.serialAlloc fun k => ↑(q k)) (AnvBandit.Vser M T) (Eq.symm hmax))));
          Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ AnvBandit.Vstar0 M) (Eq.symm hval)))
            (Finset.le_sup' (AnvBandit.J0 M) (Finset.mem_univ fun _i t => v t)))
theorem AnvBandit.benchmark_public_sandwich : ∀ {n K T : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K),
  AnvBandit.Vser M (n * T) ≤ AnvBandit.Vstar1 M ∧ AnvBandit.Vstar1 M ≤ AnvBandit.Vser M (n * T) + ↑(n - 1) * M.A :=
fun {n K T} M => ⟨AnvBandit.Vser_le_Vstar1 M, AnvBandit.Vstar1_le_Vser M⟩
theorem AnvBandit.Vser_eq_greedy_policy : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (t : ℕ),
  AnvBandit.Vser M t =
    AnvBandit.Greedy.payoff (AnvBandit.serialMarg M) (AnvBandit.Greedy.policy ⋯ (AnvBandit.serialMarg M) t) :=
fun {n K} M t =>
  let hK :=
    have this := M.two_le_K;
    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Vser_eq_greedy_policy._proof_1 this a;
  le_antisymm
    (id
      (Finset.sup'_le (AnvBandit.comps_nonempty M t) (fun f => M.serialAlloc fun k => ↑(f k)) fun q hq =>
        Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a =>
                _a ≤
                  AnvBandit.Greedy.payoff (AnvBandit.serialMarg M)
                    (AnvBandit.Greedy.policy
                      (have this := M.two_le_K;
                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Vser_eq_greedy_policy._proof_1 this a)
                      (AnvBandit.serialMarg M) t))
              (AnvBandit.serialAlloc_eq_greedyAllocation M fun k => ↑(q k))))
          (AnvBandit.Greedy.allocation_le_greedy hK (AnvBandit.serialMarg M) (AnvBandit.serialMarg_antitone M) t
            (fun k => ↑(q k)) (Finset.mem_filter.mp hq).right)))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ AnvBandit.Vser M t)
          (AnvBandit.Greedy.payoff_eq_allocation (AnvBandit.serialMarg M)
            (AnvBandit.Greedy.policy
              (have this := M.two_le_K;
              Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Vser_eq_greedy_policy._proof_1 this a)
              (AnvBandit.serialMarg M) t))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ AnvBandit.Vser M t)
            (Eq.symm
              (AnvBandit.serialAlloc_eq_greedyAllocation M
                (AnvBandit.Greedy.counts
                  (AnvBandit.Greedy.policy
                    (have this := M.two_le_K;
                    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Vser_eq_greedy_policy._proof_1 this a)
                    (AnvBandit.serialMarg M) t))))))
        (AnvBandit.le_Vser M
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a = t)
                (AnvBandit.Greedy.counts_total
                  (AnvBandit.Greedy.policy
                    (have this := M.two_le_K;
                    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Vser_eq_greedy_policy._proof_1 this a)
                    (AnvBandit.serialMarg M) t))))
            (Eq.mpr
              (id
                (congrArg (fun _a => _a = t)
                  (AnvBandit.Greedy.policy_length
                    (have this := M.two_le_K;
                    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Vser_eq_greedy_policy._proof_1 this a)
                    (AnvBandit.serialMarg M) t)))
              (Eq.refl t))))))
'AnvBandit.Vstar0_eq_n_Vser' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.benchmark_public_sandwich' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.Vser_eq_greedy_policy' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
theorem AnvBandit.PublicDP.W_eq_Vstar1 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (T : ℕ),
  AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M T 0 = AnvBandit.Vstar1 M :=
fun {n K} M T =>
  le_antisymm
    (Exists.casesOn (AnvBandit.PublicDP.W_attained M T 0) fun us h =>
      And.casesOn h fun hlen hv =>
        have hlist :=
          Eq.ndrec (motive := fun T =>
            AnvBandit.PublicDP.path M 0 us = AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M T 0 → ∃ v, List.ofFn v = us)
            (fun hv => Exists.intro us.get (List.ofFn_get us)) hlen hv;
        Exists.casesOn hlist fun v hvlist =>
          Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ AnvBandit.Vstar1 M) (Eq.symm hv)))
            (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => AnvBandit.PublicDP.path M 0 _a ≤ AnvBandit.Vstar1 M) (Eq.symm hvlist)))
              (Eq.mpr
                (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ AnvBandit.Vstar1 M) (AnvBandit.PublicDP.path_eq_J1 M fun i t => v t i)))
                (Finset.le_sup' (AnvBandit.J1 M) (Finset.mem_univ fun i t => v t i)))))
    (id
      (Finset.sup'_le (AnvBandit.Vstar0._proof_2 M) (AnvBandit.J1 M) fun u a =>
        Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ≤ AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M T 0) (Eq.symm (AnvBandit.PublicDP.path_eq_J1 M u))))
          (Eq.mp
            (congrArg (LE.le (AnvBandit.PublicDP.path M 0 (List.ofFn fun t i => u i t)))
              (congrFun' (congrArg (AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M) List.length_ofFn) 0))
            (AnvBandit.PublicDP.path_le_W M (List.ofFn fun t i => u i t) 0))))
theorem AnvBandit.PublicDP.W_batch : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (r : ℕ) (m : Fin K → ℕ),
  AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M (r + 1) m =
    (AnvBandit.comps K n).sup' ⋯ fun x =>
      ∑ k, ↑↑(x k) * M.mean k (1 + m k) + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + fun k => ↑(x k)) :=
fun {n K} M r m =>
  le_antisymm
    (id
      (Finset.sup'_le (Exists.intro (fun x => M.star) (Finset.mem_univ fun x => M.star))
        (fun u => AnvBandit.PublicDP.reward M m u + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + AnvBandit.PublicDP.count u))
        fun u a =>
        have hb := fun k =>
          have h := Finset.single_le_sum (fun k x => Nat.zero_le (AnvBandit.PublicDP.count u k)) (Finset.mem_univ k);
          Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
            AnvBandit.PublicDP.W_batch._proof_1_1 u k
              (Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => AnvBandit.PublicDP.count u k ≤ _a) (AnvBandit.PublicDP.count_total u)) h) a;
        let x := fun k => ⟨AnvBandit.PublicDP.count u k, hb k⟩;
        have hx := Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ x, AnvBandit.PublicDP.count_total u⟩;
        Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a =>
                _a + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + AnvBandit.PublicDP.count u) ≤
                  (AnvBandit.comps K n).sup' (AnvBandit.comps_nonempty M n) fun x =>
                    ∑ k, ↑↑(x k) * M.mean k (1 + m k) + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + fun k => ↑(x k)))
              (AnvBandit.PublicDP.reward_eq_batch M m u)))
          (Eq.mp
            (congrFun'
              (congrArg LE.le
                (congrFun'
                  (congrArg HAdd.hAdd
                    (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                      Eq.refl (↑(AnvBandit.PublicDP.count u x) * M.mean x (1 + m x))))
                  (AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + fun k => AnvBandit.PublicDP.count u k))))
              ((AnvBandit.comps K n).sup' (Exists.intro x hx) fun x =>
                ∑ k, ↑↑(x k) * M.mean k (1 + m k) + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + fun k => ↑(x k))))
            (Finset.le_sup'
              (fun x => ∑ k, ↑↑(x k) * M.mean k (1 + m k) + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + fun k => ↑(x k))) hx))))
    (Finset.sup'_le (AnvBandit.comps_nonempty M n)
      (fun x => ∑ k, ↑↑(x k) * M.mean k (1 + m k) + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + fun k => ↑(x k))) fun x hx =>
      Exists.casesOn (AnvBandit.exists_sched_with_counts (fun k => ↑(x k)) n (Finset.mem_filter.mp hx).right)
        fun u hu =>
        have hc := funext hu;
        have hv :=
          Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a = ∑ k, ↑↑(x k) * M.mean k (1 + m k)) (AnvBandit.PublicDP.reward_eq_batch M m u)))
            (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => ∑ k, ↑(_a k) * M.mean k (1 + m k) = ∑ k, ↑↑(x k) * M.mean k (1 + m k)) hc))
              (Eq.refl (∑ k, ↑((fun k => ↑(x k)) k) * M.mean k (1 + m k))));
        Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg (fun _a => _a + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + fun k => ↑(x k)) ≤ AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M (r + 1) m)
              (Eq.symm hv)))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg
                (fun _a =>
                  AnvBandit.PublicDP.reward M m u + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + _a) ≤
                    AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M (r + 1) m)
                (Eq.symm hc)))
            (Finset.le_sup'
              (fun u => AnvBandit.PublicDP.reward M m u + AnvBandit.PublicDP.W M r (m + AnvBandit.PublicDP.count u))
              (Finset.mem_univ u))))
'AnvBandit.PublicDP.W_eq_Vstar1' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.PublicDP.W_batch' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
theorem AnvBandit.Vpot_recursion_of_irreducible : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hd : 0 < n - 1},
  2 ≤ n →
    ∀ (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) {i0 : Fin n} {A : Finset (Fin n)},
      i0 ∈ A →
        AnvBandit.Vpot hd C i0 A =
          ↑(Finset.univ \ A).card +
            ∫ (J : Fin n → Fin n), AnvBandit.Vpot hd C i0 (AnvBandit.infStep J A) ∂AnvBandit.stepDist C :=
fun {n} {hd} hn C {i0} {A} hA => AnvBandit.Vpot_recursion hn C (AnvBandit.ReachData.ofIrreducible C.irreducible i0) hA
'AnvBandit.Vpot_recursion_of_irreducible' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
def AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n → AnvBandit.Props :=
fun {n K} M p C =>
  {
    Pβ :=
      min (↑p.T)
        (AnvBandit.Pbeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
          (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β),
    Aβ :=
      AnvBandit.Abeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H) (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p))
        p.β ↑p.T,
    M1 := AnvBandit.M1 ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star),
    DP := AnvBandit.DP ⋯ ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star),
    Kβ :=
      AnvBandit.concreteKbeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H) p.β
        (AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p) }
def AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n → Fin 2 → (Fin K → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun {n K} M p C lam c =>
  AnvBandit.Ecost (AnvBandit.Eglob n (M.supOthers c) (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).Pβ (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam))
    (AnvBandit.Enode (M.supOthers c) (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).Aβ
      (M.Clam c p.L (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).DP lam))
    (AnvBandit.Epot (M.Flam c p.L lam) (M.supOthers c) (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).M1
      (M.Flam (AnvBandit.Absorbing.unitCost M) p.L lam) (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).Kβ)
def AnvBandit.Main.canonicalContact : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K →
    (P : Fin n → Fin n → ℝ) →
      (∀ (i j : Fin n), 0 ≤ P i j) →
        (∀ (i : Fin n), ∑ j, P i j = 1) →
          (∀ (a b : Fin n), Relation.ReflTransGen (fun x y => 0 < P x y) a b) → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n :=
fun {n K} p P hP hrow hirr => AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible ⋯ P hP hrow ⋯
theorem AnvBandit.Main.theorem_C_4 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K)
  (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (c : Fin K → ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 ≤ c k) →
    c M.star = 0 →
      AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c ≤
        min (↑n * M.supOthers c * ↑p.T) (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c + p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers c * ↑p.T)) :=
fun {n K} M p C lam c hc hstar =>
  have hm := AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.weight_le_sup M c hc hstar;
  have ht := AnvBandit.GlobalTheorem.weightedLoss_trivial M p C lam c (M.supOthers c) hc hm;
  have hg := AnvBandit.Main.goodLoss_three_branches M p C lam c hc hstar;
  have hf := AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss_le_goodLoss_add_failure M p C lam c (M.supOthers c) hc hm;
  le_min ht
    (le_of_not_gt fun a =>
      Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
        (Eq.mp
          (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c) rfl
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg
                            (fun _a =>
                              AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                        (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c) rfl
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg
                            (fun _a =>
                              AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                        (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                              (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                        (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                          (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c) rfl
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg
                            (fun _a =>
                              AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                        (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c) rfl
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrArg
                              (fun _a =>
                                AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                  AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                              (Eq.symm rfl)))
                          (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf p.δ rfl

                            (id
                              (congrArg (fun _a => p.δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = p.δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)

                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                        (p.δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                            (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (↑p.T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))) +
                          0))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c) rfl
                    (Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congrArg
                          (fun _a =>
                            AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                              AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                          (Eq.symm rfl)))
                      (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c)
                        (Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                            (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                      Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                      (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                        (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                          (p.δ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (↑p.T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))) +
                            0))))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c)
                    (Nat.rawCast 1)
                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                        (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c)
                      (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero p.δ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (M.supOthers c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (↑p.T) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0)))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
          (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_le_of_neg
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_nonpos (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hg)
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hf))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a))))
theorem AnvBandit.Main.theorem_4_1 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K)
  (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2),
  AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M p C lam ≤
    AnvBandit.Uco (↑n * ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam) (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)
      (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam)) (p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)) :=
fun {n K} M p C lam =>
  have hw :=
    AnvBandit.Main.theorem_C_4 M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam) (AnvBandit.DecayModel.rho_nonneg M p.T lam)
      (AnvBandit.DecayModel.rho_star M p.T lam);
  have hr := AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret_conversion M p C lam;
  id
    (le_min (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret_trivial M p C lam)
      (le_of_not_gt fun a =>
        Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
          (Eq.mp
            (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr

                        (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam)) rfl

                          (Eq.refl

                              (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                        (min (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)

                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                        (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M p C lam) (Nat.rawCast 1)

                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))

            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_le_of_neg
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_nonpos (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hw)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hr))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a)))))
theorem AnvBandit.Main.theorem_4_1_canonical : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (P : Fin n → Fin n → ℝ) (hP : ∀ (i j : Fin n), 0 ≤ P i j)
  (hrow : ∀ (i : Fin n), ∑ j, P i j = 1) (hirr : ∀ (a b : Fin n), Relation.ReflTransGen (fun x y => 0 < P x y) a b)
  (lam : Fin 2),
  have C := AnvBandit.Main.canonicalContact p P hP hrow hirr;
  AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M p C lam ≤
    AnvBandit.Uco (↑n * ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam) (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)
      (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam)) (p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)) :=
fun {n K} M p P hP hrow hirr lam =>
  AnvBandit.Main.theorem_4_1 M p (AnvBandit.Main.canonicalContact p P hP hrow hirr) lam
theorem AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.original_theorem_4_1 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2)
  (ν : MeasureTheory.Measure (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n)) [inst : MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν],
  MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe 0) ν =
      MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe 0) (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.observedMeasure M p C lam) →
    (∀ t < p.T,
        ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib (fun ω => (ω (t + 1)).1) (Preorder.frestrictLe t)
              ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe t) ν]
          ⇑(AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.rewardKernel M p lam t)) →
      (∀ t < p.T,
          AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (t + 1) →
            ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib (fun ω => (ω (t + 1)).2)
                  (fun ω => (Preorder.frestrictLe t ω, (ω (t + 1)).1))
                  ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map (fun ω => (Preorder.frestrictLe t ω, (ω (t + 1)).1)) ν]
              ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.const (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Prefix n t × (Fin n → Bool))
                  (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.contactDistribution C).toMeasure)) →
        (∀ t < p.T,
            ¬AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (t + 1) →
              ∀ᵐ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n) ∂ν, (ω (t + 1)).2 = id) →
          AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.originalTeamRegret M p lam ν ≤
            AnvBandit.Uco (↑n * ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam) (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)
              (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam))
              (p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)) :=
fun {n K} M p C lam ν [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν] hzero hx hj habsent =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          _a ≤
            AnvBandit.Uco (↑n * ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam) (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)
              (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam)) (p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)))
        (AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.original_teamRegret_eq M p C lam ν hzero hx hj habsent)))
    (AnvBandit.Main.theorem_4_1 M p C lam)
theorem AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.original_theorem_C_4 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2)
  (ν : MeasureTheory.Measure (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n)) [inst : MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν],
  MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe 0) ν =
      MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe 0) (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.observedMeasure M p C lam) →
    (∀ t < p.T,
        ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib (fun ω => (ω (t + 1)).1) (Preorder.frestrictLe t)
              ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe t) ν]
          ⇑(AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.rewardKernel M p lam t)) →
      (∀ t < p.T,
          AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (t + 1) →
            ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib (fun ω => (ω (t + 1)).2)
                  (fun ω => (Preorder.frestrictLe t ω, (ω (t + 1)).1))
                  ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map (fun ω => (Preorder.frestrictLe t ω, (ω (t + 1)).1)) ν]
              ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.const (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Prefix n t × (Fin n → Bool))
                  (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.contactDistribution C).toMeasure)) →
        (∀ t < p.T,
            ¬AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (t + 1) →
              ∀ᵐ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n) ∂ν, (ω (t + 1)).2 = id) →
          ∀ (c : Fin K → ℝ),
            (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 ≤ c k) →
              c M.star = 0 →
                ∫ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n), AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.pathCost p c ω ∂ν ≤
                  min (↑n * M.supOthers c * ↑p.T)
                    (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c + p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers c * ↑p.T)) :=
fun {n K} M p C lam ν [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν] hzero hx hj habsent c hc hstar =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          _a ≤
            min (↑n * M.supOthers c * ↑p.T)
              (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c + p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers c * ↑p.T)))
        (AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.original_weightedLoss_eq M p C lam ν hzero hx hj habsent c)))
    (AnvBandit.Main.theorem_C_4 M p C lam c hc hstar)
'AnvBandit.Main.theorem_C_4' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.Main.theorem_4_1' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.Main.theorem_4_1_canonical' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.original_theorem_4_1' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.original_theorem_C_4' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
theorem AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_explicit_canonical : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (P : Fin n → Fin n → ℝ) (hP : ∀ (i j : Fin n), 0 ≤ P i j) (hrow : ∀ (i : Fin n), ∑ j, P i j = 1)
  (hirr : ∀ (a b : Fin n), Relation.ReflTransGen (fun x y => 0 < P y x) a b) (lam : Fin 2) (T h : ℕ) (ξ β : ℝ)
  (hT : 1 ≤ T) (hh : 1 ≤ h) (hξ : 0 < ξ) (hβ : 1 < β),
  have C := AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible ⋯ P hP hrow hirr;
  AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M (AnvBandit.Execution.explicitParams M T h ξ β hT hh hξ hβ) C lam ≤
    AnvBandit.explicitBound M T h ξ β (AnvBandit.epsP C) lam :=
fun {n K} M P hP hrow hirr lam T h ξ β hT hh hξ hβ =>
  AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_explicit M
    (AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible
      (have this := M.two_le_n;
      Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_explicit_canonical._proof_1 T h this a)
      P hP hrow hirr)
    lam T h ξ β hT hh hξ hβ
theorem AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_time_order : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (β : ℝ) (hβ : 1 < β),
  ∃ A,
    0 ≤ A ∧
      ∀ (T h H : ℕ) (hT : 1 ≤ T) (hh : 1 ≤ h) (hH : 1 ≤ H) (hdiv : h ∣ H),
        AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M (AnvBandit.Execution.confidenceParams M T h H β hT hh hH hdiv hβ) C
            lam ≤
          A * (↑H + Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ (β / (β - 1)) / ↑H ^ (β / (β - 1) - 1) + Real.log (↑T + 1) + 1) :=
fun {n K} M C lam β hβ =>
  let a := |M.Gamma lam| + |M.Dlam lam| + ↑n * M.rhoMax lam / 4;
  let b := AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.timePropagationCoefficient M C lam β;
  let c := M.Jlam lam * AnvBandit.logConfidenceCoefficient n K;
  have hn :=
    have this := M.two_le_n;
    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_time_order._proof_1_1 this a;
  have hK :=
    have this := M.two_le_K;
    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_time_order._proof_1_2 this a;
  have ha :=
    have hρ := AnvBandit.DecayModel.rhoMax_nonneg M lam;
    id
      (add_nonneg (add_nonneg (abs_nonneg (M.Gamma lam)) (abs_nonneg (M.Dlam lam)))
        (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.div_nonneg_of_nonneg_of_pos
          (mul_nonneg
            (le_of_lt
              (Nat.cast_pos'.mpr
                (lt_of_lt_of_le
                  (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℕ (Eq.refl 1))
                    (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 1)))
                  hn)))
            hρ)
          (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 4))
            (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 4)))));
  have hb := AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.timePropagationCoefficient_nonneg M C lam hβ;
  have hc :=
    mul_nonneg (AnvBandit.DecayModel.Jlam_nonneg M hn hK lam) (AnvBandit.logConfidenceCoefficient_nonneg hn hK);
  Exists.intro (a + b + c)
    ⟨add_nonneg (add_nonneg ha hb) hc, fun T h H hT hh hH hdiv =>
      have hbudget := AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_general_budget M C lam T h H β hT hh hH hdiv hβ;
      have hlog :=
        Real.log_nonneg
          (cast
            (Eq.symm
              (Eq.trans (congr (congrArg LE.le (Eq.symm Nat.cast_one)) (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd ↑T) (Eq.symm Nat.cast_one)))
                (Eq.trans (congrArg (LE.le ↑1) (Nat.cast_add._simp_1 T 1)) Nat.cast_le._simp_1)))
            (have this :=
              Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_time_order._proof_1_3 T h H hT a;
            this));
      have hq :=
        le_of_lt
          (add_pos_of_pos_of_nonneg
            (Nat.cast_pos'.mpr
              (lt_of_lt_of_le
                (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℕ (Eq.refl 1))
                  (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 1)))
                hH))
            (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.div_nonneg_of_nonneg_of_pos (Real.rpow_nonneg hlog (β / (β - 1)))
              (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos
                (Nat.cast_pos'.mpr
                  (lt_of_lt_of_le
                    (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℕ (Eq.refl 1))
                      (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 1)))
                    hH))
                (β / (β - 1) - 1))));
      have h0 :=
        id
          (le_of_not_gt fun a =>
            Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
              (Eq.mp
                (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 4)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf |M.Gamma lam| rfl
                                  (Eq.mpr
                                    (id
                                      (congrArg
                                        (fun _a =>
                                          |M.Gamma lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                            |M.Gamma lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                        (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                    (Eq.refl (|M.Gamma lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right |M.Gamma lam| (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 4)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 4))))

                                    (|M.Gamma lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 + 0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 4)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf |M.Dlam lam| rfl

                                                              (Eq.refl (Nat.mul 1 1)) (Eq.refl 4))))
                                                        (Eq.refl (Nat.mul 4 1)) (Eq.refl 4))))))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 4))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                  0)))
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                              (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * NNRat.rawCast 1 4) +
                                                0))
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                              (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                0))))
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                                          (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                            (|M.Gamma lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 +
                                              (|M.Dlam lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 + 0)))))
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (M.rhoMax lam) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt
                                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul |M.Gamma lam| (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
