                                          (M.Gamma lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4)
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                            (M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4)
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (M.rhoMax lam)
                                                  (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                                      (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                                (|M.Gamma lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 4).rawCast +
                                                  (|M.Dlam lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 4).rawCast + 0))))))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                      (b ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 4).rawCast)
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                        (M.Jlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          (AnvBandit.logConfidenceCoefficient n K ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                            (Int.negOfNat 4).rawCast))
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                          (M.Gamma lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 +
                                            (M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 +
                                              (|M.Gamma lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 4).rawCast +
                                                (|M.Dlam lam| ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 4).rawCast + 0))))))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 4)))
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf
                                        (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M
                                          (AnvBandit.Execution.confidenceParams M T h H β hT hh hH hdiv hβ) C lam)
                                        rfl
                                        (Eq.mpr
                                          (id
                                            (congrArg
                                              (fun _a =>
                                                AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M
                                                        (AnvBandit.Execution.confidenceParams M T h H β hT hh hH hdiv
                                                          hβ)
                                                        C lam ^
                                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                                    Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                                  AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M

                                                          hβ)
                                                        C lam ^
                                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                                    _a)
                                              (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                          (Eq.refl
                                            (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M

                                                  lam ^
                                                Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                              Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                            (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M
                                              (AnvBandit.Execution.confidenceParams M T h H β hT hh hH hdiv hβ) C lam)
                                            (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 4)
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 4))))
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 4))

                                            (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M

                                            0))
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                          (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M
                                                  (AnvBandit.Execution.confidenceParams M T h H β hT hh hH hdiv hβ) C
                                                  lam ^
                                                Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                              Nat.rawCast 4 +
                                            0))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 4)))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.Gamma lam) rfl
                                                (Eq.mpr
                                                  (id

                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (M.Gamma lam) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 4)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 4))))
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 4))
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                    (M.Gamma lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 + 0)))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                                  (M.Gamma lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                  (M.Gamma lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 + 0))))
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 4)))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.Dlam lam) rfl
                                                (Eq.mpr
                                                  (id
                                                    (congrArg
                                                      (fun _a =>
                                                        M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                                          M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                                      (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                                  (Eq.refl (M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (M.Dlam lam) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 4)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 4))))
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 4))
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                    (M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 + 0)))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                                  (M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                  (M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 + 0))))
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                              (M.Gamma lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4)
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                                (M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4 + 0))))
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (↑n) rfl
                                                (Eq.mpr
                                                  (id
                                                    (congrArg
                                                      (fun _a =>
                                                        ↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                                      (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                                  (Eq.refl (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1))))
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                    (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                                  (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                  (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.rhoMax lam) rfl
                                                (Eq.mpr
                                                  (id
                                                    (congrArg
                                                      (fun _a =>
                                                        M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                                          M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                                      (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                                  (Eq.refl (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (M.rhoMax lam)
                                                    (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1))))
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                    (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                                  (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                  (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (M.rhoMax lam)
                                                    (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1)))))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero
                                                  (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                  (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                    0)))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                                (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                                (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (M.rhoMax lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                                  0))))
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                            (M.Gamma lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4)
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                              (M.Dlam lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 4)
                                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                                (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (⋯ ^ ⋯ * ⋯) + ⋯)))))
                                        ⋯ ⋯)
                                      ⋯ ⋯)
                                    ⋯)
                                  ⋯)
                                ⋯ ⋯)
                              ⋯ ⋯)
                            ⋯ ⋯)
                          ⋯ ⋯)
                        ⋯ ⋯)
                      ⋯))
                  ⋯))
          ⋯)⟩
theorem AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_Hxi_order : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (ξ β : ℝ) (hξ : 0 < ξ) (hβ : 1 < β),
  ∃ A,
    0 ≤ A ∧
      ∀ (T h : ℕ) (hT : 1 ≤ T) (hh : 1 ≤ h),
        AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M (AnvBandit.Execution.explicitParams M T h ξ β hT hh hξ hβ) C lam ≤
          A * (↑h + Real.log (↑T + 1)) :=
fun {n K} M C lam ξ β hξ hβ =>
  let ch := AnvBandit.Ch (↑n) (M.rhoMax lam) (M.Abetaxi ξ β (AnvBandit.epsP C));
  let cl := AnvBandit.Clog ξ ch (M.Jlam lam);
  let c0 := AnvBandit.C0 (M.Gamma lam) (M.Dlam lam) (↑n * M.rhoMax lam) cl (↑n * ↑K);
  have hn :=
    have this := M.two_le_n;
    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_Hxi_order._proof_1_1 this a;
  have hK :=
    have this := M.two_le_K;
    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_Hxi_order._proof_1_2 this a;
  have hch :=
    id
      (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg n) (AnvBandit.DecayModel.rhoMax_nonneg M lam))
        (AnvBandit.DecayModel.Abetaxi_nonneg M hn
          (lt_of_not_ge fun a =>
            Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
              (Eq.mp
                (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))
                            Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf β rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg (fun _a => β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul β (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                    (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                              Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                (β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                              (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                            (β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf β rfl
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg (fun _a => β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                (Eq.symm rfl)))
                            (Eq.refl (β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (β ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero β (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                              (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0)))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_neg_of_le
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_neg (neg_neg_of_pos Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.zero_lt_one)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt hβ))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le a))))
          (AnvBandit.epsP_pos C)));
  have hcl :=
    id
      (mul_nonneg
        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_le_true (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero)
          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 3)) (Eq.refl true))
        (add_nonneg (mul_nonneg (LT.lt.le hξ) hch) (AnvBandit.DecayModel.Jlam_nonneg M hn hK lam)));
  Exists.intro (|c0| + ch + cl)
    ⟨add_nonneg (add_nonneg (abs_nonneg c0) hch) hcl, fun T h hT hh =>
      have hb :=
        LE.le.trans (AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_explicit M C lam T h ξ β hT hh hξ hβ)
          (min_le_right (↑n * ↑T)
            (AnvBandit.C0 (M.Gamma lam) (M.Dlam lam) (↑n * M.rhoMax lam)
                  (AnvBandit.Clog ξ (AnvBandit.Ch (↑n) (M.rhoMax lam) (M.Abetaxi ξ β (AnvBandit.epsP C))) (M.Jlam lam))
                  (↑n * ↑K) +

                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf ch rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg (fun _a => ch ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ch ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (ch ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.log (↑T + 1)) rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg
                                    (fun _a =>
                                      Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                        Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left ch (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (Real.log (↑T + 1)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                        (Eq.refl 1)))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (ch ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                  (ch ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (ch ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul ch (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (Real.log (↑T + 1)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                      (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                              Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                              (ch ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) +
                                0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero ch (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (Real.log (↑T + 1)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                            (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (cl ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf cl rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg (fun _a => cl ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = cl ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (cl ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (↑h) rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg (fun _a => ↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (↑h) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left cl (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                      (Eq.refl 1)))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (cl ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (cl ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0)))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                              (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (cl ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (↑h) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul cl (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                    (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                            Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                            (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (cl ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) + 0))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (↑h) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero cl (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0)))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_neg_of_le
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_neg_of_le
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_neg_of_le
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_le_of_neg
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_nonpos (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hb)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le hconstant))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le (mul_nonneg (abs_nonneg c0) hlog)))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le (mul_nonneg hch hlog)))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le
                    (mul_nonneg hcl
                      (have this :=
                        le_of_lt
                          (Nat.cast_pos'.mpr
                            (lt_of_lt_of_le
                              (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℕ (Eq.refl 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 1)))
                              hh));
                      this))))))
        hb⟩
theorem AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_fixed_h_log_order : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (ξ β : ℝ) (hξ : 0 < ξ) (hβ : 1 < β) (h : ℕ) (hh : 1 ≤ h),
  ∃ B,
    0 ≤ B ∧
      ∀ (T : ℕ) (hT : 1 ≤ T),
        AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M (AnvBandit.Execution.explicitParams M T h ξ β hT hh hξ hβ) C lam ≤
          B * Real.log (↑T + 1) :=
fun {n K} M C lam ξ β hξ hβ h hh =>
  Exists.casesOn (AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_Hxi_order M C lam ξ β hξ hβ) fun A h_1 =>
    And.casesOn h_1 fun hA hb =>
      have hlog2 :=
        Real.log_pos
          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_lt_true (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one)
            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 2)) (Eq.refl false));
      Exists.intro (A * (↑h / Real.log 2 + 1))
        ⟨mul_nonneg hA
            (le_of_lt
              (add_pos'
                (div_pos
                  (Nat.cast_pos'.mpr
                    (lt_of_lt_of_le
                      (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℕ (Eq.refl 1))
                        (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 1)))
                      hh))
                  (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.log_pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 2))
                    (Eq.refl true)))
                (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one)
                  (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 1))))),
          fun T hT =>
          have hl :=
            Real.log_le_log
              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_lt_true (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero)
                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 2)) (Eq.refl false))
              (cast
                (Eq.symm
                  (Eq.trans (congrArg (LE.le ↑2) (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd ↑T) (Eq.symm Nat.cast_one)))
                    (Eq.trans (congrArg (LE.le ↑2) (Nat.cast_add._simp_1 T 1)) Nat.cast_le._simp_1)))
                (have this :=
                  Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                    AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_fixed_h_log_order._proof_1_1 h T hT a;
                this));
          have hhlog :=
            Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => ↑h ≤ _a) (div_mul_eq_mul_div (↑h) (Real.log 2) (Real.log (↑T + 1)))))
              (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a) (propext (le_div_iff₀ hlog2))))
                (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hl (Nat.cast_nonneg h)));
          have he := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (add_le_add_left hhlog (Real.log (↑T + 1))) hA;
          have hmain := hb T h hT hh;
          le_of_not_gt fun a =>
            Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
              (Eq.mp
                (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf A rfl
                                (Eq.mpr
                                  (id
                                    (congrArg (fun _a => A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                      (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                  (Eq.refl (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right A (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                        (Eq.refl 1))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (↑h) rfl
                                  (Eq.mpr
                                    (id
                                      (congrArg (fun _a => ↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                        (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                    (Eq.refl (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (↑h) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                      (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.log (↑T + 1)) rfl
                                  (Eq.mpr
                                    (id
                                      (congrArg
                                        (fun _a =>
                                          Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                            Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                        (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                    (Eq.refl (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (Real.log (↑T + 1)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                      (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                    (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                  (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left A (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (↑h) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                        (Eq.refl 1)))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left A (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (Real.log (↑T + 1)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1)))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                    (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                                  (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                                    (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                  (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                  (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Real.log (↑T + 1) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0)))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf A rfl
                                (Eq.mpr
                                  (id
                                    (congrArg (fun _a => A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                      (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                  (Eq.refl (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right A (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                        (Eq.refl 1))))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (Nat.rawCast 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (A ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.div_congr
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (↑h) rfl
                                      (Eq.mpr
                                        (id
                                          (congrArg
                                            (fun _a => ↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                        (Eq.refl (↑h ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.log 2) rfl
                                      (Eq.mpr
                                        (id
                                          (congrArg
                                            (fun _a =>
                                              Real.log 2 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                                Real.log 2 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                        (Eq.refl (Real.log 2 ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.div_pf
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.inv_single
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.inv_mul (Eq.refl (Real.log 2)⁻¹)
                                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNNRat.to_isNat
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNNRat_inv_pos
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isNNRat
                                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)))))
                                          (Eq.symm
                                              (Eq.mpr
                                                (id
                                                  (congrArg
                                                    (fun _a =>
                                                      (Real.log 2)⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                                        (Real.log 2)⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                                (Eq.refl ((Real.log 2)⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))) ▸
                                            Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (Real.log 2)⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Eq.refl 1))))))
                                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (↑h) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (Real.log 2)⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq

                                        (Eq.symm
                                            (Eq.mpr

                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a))))⟩
theorem AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_sublinear : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (ξ β : ℝ) (hξ : 0 < ξ) (hβ : 1 < β) (h : ℕ → ℕ)
  (hh : ∀ (T : ℕ), 1 ≤ h (T + 1)),
  ((fun T => ↑(h T)) =o[Filter.atTop] fun T => ↑T) →
    Filter.Tendsto
      (fun T =>
        AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M
            (AnvBandit.Execution.explicitParams M (T + 1) (h (T + 1)) ξ β ⋯ ⋯ hξ hβ) C lam /
          (↑n * (↑T + 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0) :=
fun {n K} M C lam ξ β hξ hβ h hh hsub =>
  Exists.casesOn (AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.actual_Hxi_order M C lam ξ β hξ hβ) fun A h_1 =>
    And.casesOn h_1 fun hA hbound =>
      have hhlim :=
        Eq.mp
          (congrFun'
            (congrFun'
              (congrArg Filter.Tendsto
                (funext fun x =>
                  congrArg (HDiv.hDiv ↑(h (x + 1)))
                    (Eq.trans (Nat.cast_add x 1) (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd ↑x) Nat.cast_one))))
              Filter.atTop)
            (nhds 0))
          (Filter.Tendsto.comp (Asymptotics.IsLittleO.tendsto_div_nhds_zero hsub) (Filter.tendsto_add_atTop_nat 1));
      have hlim :=
        Eq.mp
          (congrArg
            (Filter.Tendsto (fun x => A / ↑n * (↑(h (x + 1)) / (↑x + 1) + Real.log (↑x + 2) / (↑x + 1))) Filter.atTop)
            (congrArg nhds (Eq.trans (congrArg (HMul.hMul (A / ↑n)) (add_zero 0)) (mul_zero (A / ↑n)))))
          (Filter.Tendsto.const_mul (A / ↑n)
            (Filter.Tendsto.add hhlim AnvBandit.ActualExplicitRegret.horizon_log_ratio));
      have hn :=
        cast (Eq.symm (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg LT.lt (Eq.symm Nat.cast_zero)) ↑n) Nat.cast_lt._simp_1))
          (have this :=
            have this := M.two_le_n;

                                  (Nat.rawCast 1 + (↑T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)

                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                      (Eq.refl 1)))))

                                  0)))

                              ((Nat.rawCast 1 + (↑T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *

                                0))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero

                                    Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (Real.log (↑T + 2)) rfl

                                    Real.log (↑T + 2) ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =

                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (↑T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf'
                              (Eq.refl (Nat.rawCast 1 + (↑T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))⁻¹) rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id

                                    (fun _a =>
                                      (Nat.rawCast 1 + (↑T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *

                                        (Nat.rawCast 1 + (↑T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))⁻¹ ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *

                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (Real.log (↑T + 2)) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                  (Nat.rawCast 1 + (↑T ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))⁻¹ (Nat.rawCast 1)

                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)

                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul

theorem AnvBandit.BaselineRealizedReward.original_realized_regret_bound : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.Params n K) (lam : Fin 2) (ν : MeasureTheory.Measure (AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.Path n))
  [inst : MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν],
  MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe 0) ν =
      MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe 0) (AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.executionMeasure M p lam) →
    (∀ t < p.T,
        ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib (fun ω => (ω (t + 1)).1) (Preorder.frestrictLe t)
              ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map (Preorder.frestrictLe t) ν]
          ⇑(AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.rewardKernel M p lam t)) →
      (∀ t < p.T, ∀ᵐ (ω : AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.Path n) ∂ν, (ω (t + 1)).2 = id) →
        AnvBandit.BaselineTrajectory.oracle M p.T lam -
            ∫ (ω : AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.Path n), AnvBandit.BaselineRealizedReward.realizedTotal p ω ∂ν ≤
          AnvBandit.Uind (↑n * ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam) (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)
            (M.Flam (M.rho p.T lam) (AnvBandit.Execution.Params.L p) lam)
            (p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)) :=
fun {n K} M p lam ν [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν] hzero hx habsent =>
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          AnvBandit.BaselineTrajectory.oracle M p.T lam - _a ≤
            AnvBandit.Uind (↑n * ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam) (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)
              (M.Flam (M.rho p.T lam) (AnvBandit.Execution.Params.L p) lam)
              (p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)))
        (AnvBandit.BaselineRealizedReward.expected_realized_eq_pathReward_original M p lam ν hzero hx habsent)))
    (AnvBandit.BaselineObserved.original_teamRegret_bound M p lam ν hzero hx habsent)
theorem AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.corollary_4_2 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2),
  AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam) < M.Flam (M.rho p.T lam) p.L lam →
    AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam) + p.δ * (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T) <
        min (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T) (↑n * ↑p.T - M.Gamma lam) →
      AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M p C lam ≤ AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.cooperativeBound M p C lam ∧
        AnvBandit.BaselineTrajectory.teamRegret M p lam ≤ AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.independentBound M p lam ∧
          AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.cooperativeBound M p C lam <
            AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.independentBound M p lam :=
fun {n K} M p C lam hcost hcut =>
  ⟨AnvBandit.Main.theorem_4_1 M p C lam,
    ⟨AnvBandit.BaselineTrajectory.teamRegret_bound M p lam,
      AnvBandit.corollary_4_2 p.T (M.Gamma lam) (↑n * M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam) * ↑p.T)
        (M.Flam (M.rho p.T lam) p.L lam) (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam)) p.δ hcost hcut⟩⟩
'AnvBandit.BaselineRealizedReward.original_realized_regret_bound' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
'AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.corollary_4_2' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
theorem AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.cooperative_choose_eq_min_argmax : ∀ {K n : ℕ} (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K)
  {i : Fin n} (s : AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState n K i),
  AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState.choose p s =
    {k ∈ s.active |
          AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.extendedIndex s.N (AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState.index p s) k =
            s.active.sup' ⋯ (AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.extendedIndex s.N (AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState.index p s))}.min'
      ⋯ :=
fun {K n} p {i} s =>
  AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.priorityChoice_eq_min_argmax s.active (AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState.active_nonempty s) s.N
    (AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState.index p s)
theorem AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.baseline_choose_eq_min_argmax : ∀ {K n : ℕ} (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K)
  {i : Fin n} (s : AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState n K i),
  AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState.choose p s =
    {k ∈ s.active |
          AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.extendedIndex s.N (AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState.index p s) k =
            s.active.sup' ⋯
              (AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.extendedIndex s.N (AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState.index p s))}.min'
      ⋯ :=
fun {K n} p {i} s =>
  AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.priorityChoice_eq_min_argmax s.active
    (AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState.active_nonempty s) s.N (AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState.index p s)

theorem AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss_global_paper : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (c : Fin K → ℝ) (cmax : ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 ≤ c k) →
    c M.star = 0 →
      (∀ (k : Fin K), c k ≤ cmax) →
        AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ≤
          ↑n * cmax *
              min (↑p.T)
                (AnvBandit.Pbeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                  (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β) +
            M.Clam c p.L 0 lam :=
fun {n K} M p C lam c cmax hc hstar hcmax =>
  have h1 := AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss_global_exact M p C lam c cmax hc hstar hcmax;
  have h2 :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting_le_truncated_Pbeta M p C lam)
      (mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg n) (LE.le.trans (hc M.star) (hcmax M.star)));
  le_of_not_gt fun a =>
    Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
      (Eq.mp
        (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c) rfl
                    (Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congrArg
                          (fun _a =>
                            AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                              AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                          (Eq.symm rfl)))
                      (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (↑n) rfl
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg (fun _a => ↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                (Eq.symm rfl)))
                            (Eq.refl (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf cmax rfl
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg (fun _a => cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                (Eq.symm rfl)))
                            (Eq.refl (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right cmax (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                    (Eq.refl 1)))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                              (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0)))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                            (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1) + 0))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam) rfl
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrArg
                              (fun _a =>
                                AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                  AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                              (Eq.symm rfl)))
                          (Eq.refl
                            (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left cmax (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                    (Eq.refl 1))))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero
                            (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                            (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                              0)))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                          (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                          (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                            0))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam) rfl
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg
                            (fun _a =>
                              M.Clam c p.L 0 lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                M.Clam c p.L 0 lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                        (Eq.refl (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                      (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                        (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                          (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                        (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul cmax (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam)
                            (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
