                    (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                    Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                    (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                      (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                          (cmax ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                            (min (↑p.T)
                                  (AnvBandit.Pbeta
                                    (have this := p.hn;
                                    Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                      AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.actualTau._proof_1 this a)
                                    C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                    (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β) ^
                                Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              Nat.rawCast 1)) +
                        (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c)
                  (Nat.rawCast 1)
                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero cmax (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                        (min (↑p.T)
                          (AnvBandit.Pbeta
                            (have this := p.hn;
                            Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.actualTau._proof_1 this a)
                            C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                            (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β))
                        (Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                            (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0)))))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
        (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_le_of_neg
          (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_nonpos (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le h1)
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le h2))
          (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a)))
theorem AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.goodLoss_node : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (c : Fin K → ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 ≤ c k) →
    c M.star = 0 →
      AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ≤
        M.supOthers c *
            AnvBandit.Abeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
              (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T +
          M.Clam c p.L (AnvBandit.DP ⋯ ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star)) lam :=
fun {n K} M p C lam c hc hstar =>
  if hl : lam = 0 then
    Eq.symm hl ▸
      have h := AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.goodLoss_node_personal M p C 0 c hc hstar;
      id
        (le_of_not_gt fun a =>
          Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
            (Eq.mp
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c) rfl
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrArg
                              (fun _a =>
                                AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                  AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                              (Eq.symm rfl)))
                          (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.supOthers c) rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg
                                    (fun _a =>
                                      M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                        M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf
                              (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                (have this := p.hn;
                                Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                  AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T)
                              rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg
                                    (fun _a =>
                                      AnvBandit.Abeta
                                              (have this := p.hn;
                                              Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                                AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                              C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                              (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                        AnvBandit.Abeta
                                              (have this := p.hn;
                                              Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                                AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                              C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                              (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl
                                  (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                        (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (M.supOthers c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                    (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                      (have this := p.hn;
                                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                        AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                      C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                      (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T)
                                    (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                        (Eq.refl 1)))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                  (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                            (have this := p.hn;
                                            Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                              AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                            C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                            (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                    0)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                                (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                        (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                  0))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                          (have this := p.hn;
                                          Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                            AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                          C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                          (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                  0))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.Ccap c p.L) rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                            (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                    (have this := p.hn;
                                    Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                      AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                    C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                    (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                  Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                Nat.rawCast 1))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                              (M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.wmax c p.L) rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf
                            (AnvBandit.DP
                              (have this := p.hn;
                              Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                              p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))
                            rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    AnvBandit.DP
                                            (have this := p.hn;
                                            Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                              AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                            p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                      AnvBandit.DP
                                            (have this := p.hn;
                                            Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                              AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                            p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl
                                (AnvBandit.DP
                                      (have this := p.hn;
                                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                        AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                      p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (M.wmax c p.L) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                  (AnvBandit.DP
                                    (have this := p.hn;
                                    Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                      AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                    p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))
                                  (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                      (Eq.refl 1)))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    (AnvBandit.DP
                                          (have this := p.hn;
                                          Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                            AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                          p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                  0)))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                              (AnvBandit.DP
                                      (have this := p.hn;
                                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                        AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                      p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                0))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                              (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  (AnvBandit.DP
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                          (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                            (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                  (have this := p.hn;
                                  Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                    AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                  C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                  (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              Nat.rawCast 1))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                              (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  (AnvBandit.DP
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                0)))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (M.supOthers c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul
                              (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                (have this := p.hn;
                                Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                  AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T)
                              (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (M.Ccap c p.L) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (M.wmax c p.L) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul
                                  (AnvBandit.DP
                                    (have this := p.hn;
                                    Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                      AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                    p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))
                                  (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                      (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))))
                              Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                          (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                            (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                      (have this := p.hn;
                                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                        AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                      C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                      (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) +
                              (M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast +
                                (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    (AnvBandit.DP
                                          (have this := p.hn;
                                          Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                            AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                          p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast) +
                                  0)))))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.supOthers c) rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf
                            (AnvBandit.Abeta
                              (have this := p.hn;
                              Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                              C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                              (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T)
                            rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    AnvBandit.Abeta
                                            (have this := p.hn;
                                            Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                              AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                            C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                            (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                      AnvBandit.Abeta
                                            (have this := p.hn;
                                            Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                              AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                            C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                            (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl
                                (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                      (have this := p.hn;
                                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                        AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                      C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                      (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (M.supOthers c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                  (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                    (have this := p.hn;
                                    Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                      AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                    C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                    (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T)
                                  (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                      (Eq.refl 1)))))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                          (have this := p.hn;
                                          Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                            AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                          C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                          (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                  0)))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul
                              (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                      (have this := p.hn;
                                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                        AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                      C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                      (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                0))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                              (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                        (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.Ccap c p.L) rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf
                              (AnvBandit.DP
                                (have this := p.hn;
                                Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                  AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))
                              rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg
                                    (fun _a =>
                                      AnvBandit.DP
                                              (have this := p.hn;
                                              Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                                AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                              p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                        AnvBandit.DP
                                              (have this := p.hn;
                                              Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                                AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                              p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl
                                  (AnvBandit.DP
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.wmax c p.L) rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg
                                    (fun _a =>
                                      M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right (M.wmax c p.L) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left
                                    (AnvBandit.DP
                                      (have this := p.hn;
                                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                        AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                      p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))
                                    (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_mul (Eq.refl HMul.hMul)
                                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1) (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1)
                                        (Eq.refl 1)))))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_zero
                                  (AnvBandit.DP
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1))
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                  (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      (AnvBandit.DP
                                            (have this := p.hn;
                                            Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                              AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                            p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                        Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                    0)))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.zero_mul (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0))
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                                (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    (AnvBandit.DP
                                          (have this := p.hn;
                                          Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                            AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                          p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                  0))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                              (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  (AnvBandit.DP
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                0))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt
                          (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                            (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                  (have this := p.hn;
                                  Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                    AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                  C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                  (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                Nat.rawCast 1 *
                              Nat.rawCast 1))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                            (M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 +
                              (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  (AnvBandit.DP
                                        (have this := p.hn;
                                        Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                          AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                        p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                0)))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c) rfl
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrArg
                              (fun _a =>
                                AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                  AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                              (Eq.symm rfl)))
                          (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c)
                            (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                          Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt
                          (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                            (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                (AnvBandit.Abeta
                                      (have this := p.hn;
                                      Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                        AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                      C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                                      (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T ^
                                    Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                  Nat.rawCast 1) +
                              (M.Ccap c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 +
                                (M.wmax c p.L ^ Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    (AnvBandit.DP
                                          (have this := p.hn;
                                          Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                            AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                          p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) ^
                                        Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                      Nat.rawCast 1) +
                                  0)))))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C 0 c)
                        (Nat.rawCast 1)
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (M.supOthers c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                            (AnvBandit.Abeta
                              (have this := p.hn;
                              Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                              C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                              (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β ↑p.T)
                            (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (M.Ccap c p.L) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (M.wmax c p.L) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                                (AnvBandit.DP
                                  (have this := p.hn;
                                  Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                    AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                                  p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))
                                (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                    (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0))))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_le_of_neg (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le h)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a))))
  else
    have h1 :=
      Fin.ext
        (have hn := fun h => hl (Fin.ext h);
        have hb := lam.isLt;
        id (Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.goodLoss_node._proof_1_1 lam hn hb a));
    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun lam =>
      ¬lam = 0 →
        AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ≤
          M.supOthers c *
              AnvBandit.Abeta
                (have this := p.hn;
                Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                  AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H) (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β
                ↑p.T +
            M.Clam c p.L
              (AnvBandit.DP
                (have this := p.hn;
                Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                  AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.integrate_node_bound._proof_1✝ this a)
                p.hn C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))
              lam)
      (fun hl => AnvBandit.ActualNodeExpectation.goodLoss_node_public_min M p C c hc hstar) (Eq.symm h1) hl
theorem AnvBandit.Absorbing.weighted_loss_potential_branch : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (c : Fin K → ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), 0 ≤ c k) →
    c M.star = 0 →
      AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ≤
        M.Flam c p.L lam +
          M.supOthers c *
            (2 * AnvBandit.M1 ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) *
                M.Flam (AnvBandit.Absorbing.unitCost M) p.L lam +
              AnvBandit.concreteKbeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H) p.β
                (AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) :=
fun {n K} M p C lam c hc hstar =>
  let hd :=
    have this := p.hn;
    Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Absorbing.missing_drift_budget._proof_1 this a;
  have hm := id (Finset.sum_nonneg fun x x_1 => ENNReal.toReal_nonneg);
  have hdlen := fun ℓ =>
    Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => 0 ≤ _a) (Eq.symm (AnvBandit.Execution.Params.phaseLength_cast p ℓ))))
      (Nat.cast_nonneg' (p.phaseEnd ℓ - p.phaseEnd (ℓ - 1)));
  AnvBandit.lemma_C_3 M c p.L lam p.T (AnvBandit.dEll (↑p.H) p.β)
    (fun ℓ => AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedPotential M p C lam (ℓ - 1))
    (AnvBandit.M1 hd C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))
    (AnvBandit.concreteKbeta hd C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H) p.β
      (AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p))
    (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c) hc hm
    (of_eq_true
      (Eq.trans
        (congrFun'
          (congrArg Eq
            (Eq.trans
              (congr
                (congrArg HSub.hSub
                  (congrArg (HMul.hMul ↑p.H)
                    (congrArg Nat.cast
                      (congrArg Nat.ceil (congrFun' (congrArg HPow.hPow (CharP.cast_eq_zero ℝ 0)) p.β)))))
                (congrArg (HMul.hMul ↑p.H)
                  (congrArg Nat.cast
                    (congrArg Nat.ceil
                      (congrFun'
                        (congrArg HPow.hPow (Eq.trans (congrArg Nat.cast (zero_tsub 1)) (CharP.cast_eq_zero ℝ 0)))
                        p.β)))))
              (sub_self (↑p.H * ↑⌈0 ^ p.β⌉₊))))
          0)
        (eq_self 0)))
    hdlen (fun ℓ => AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedPotential_nonneg M p C lam (ℓ - 1))
    (have h0 := AnvBandit.Absorbing.goodLoss_le_exploration_phases M p C lam c hc hstar;
    have hmax := LE.le.trans (hc M.star) (AnvBandit.Absorbing.cost_le_supOthers M c hc hstar M.star);
    have h1 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (AnvBandit.Absorbing.missing_drift_budget M p C lam p.T) hmax;
    have hnorm := fun j =>
      Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.Absorbing.weighted_loss_potential_branch._proof_1_1 j a;
    Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg (LE.le (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c))
          (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (M.Flam c p.L lam))
            (congrArg (HMul.hMul (M.supOthers c))
              (congrArg
                (HAdd.hAdd
                  (2 * AnvBandit.M1 hd C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) *
                    M.Flam (fun k => if k = M.star then 0 else 1) p.L lam))
                (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl (Finset.range p.T)) fun x a =>
                  congrArg (HMul.hMul (AnvBandit.dEll (↑p.H) p.β (x + 1)))
                    (congr
                      (congrArg HSub.hSub
                        (congrArg (AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedPotential M p C lam) (Nat.add_sub_cancel x 1)))
                      (congrArg (AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedPotential M p C lam) ((fun j => hnorm j) x)))))))))
      (id
        (le_of_not_gt fun a =>
          Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
            (Eq.mp
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c) rfl
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg
                                (fun _a =>
                                  AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                    AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                (Eq.symm rfl)))
                            (Eq.refl (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.Flam c p.L lam) rfl
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    M.Flam c p.L lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                      M.Flam c p.L lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                  (Eq.symm rfl)))
                              (Eq.refl (M.Flam c p.L lam ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_congr
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (M.supOthers c) rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg
                                    (fun _a =>
                                      M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                        M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl (M.supOthers c ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf
                              (∑ j ∈ Finset.range p.T,
                                AnvBandit.dEll (↑p.H) p.β (j + 1) * AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedMissing M p C lam j)
                              rfl
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg
                                    (fun _a =>
                                      (∑ j ∈ Finset.range p.T,
                                              AnvBandit.dEll (↑p.H) p.β (j + 1) *
                                                AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedMissing M p C lam j) ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          Nat.rawCast 1 =
                                        (∑ j ∈ Finset.range p.T,
                                              AnvBandit.dEll (↑p.H) p.β (j + 1) *
                                                AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedMissing M p C lam j) ^
                                            Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                          _a)
                                    (Eq.symm rfl)))
                                (Eq.refl
                                  ((∑ j ∈ Finset.range p.T,
                                        AnvBandit.dEll (↑p.H) p.β (j + 1) *
                                          AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedMissing M p C lam j) ^
                                      Nat.rawCast 1 *
                                    Nat.rawCast 1))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_mul
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_add
                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_left (M.supOthers c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.mul_pf_right
                                    (∑ j ∈ Finset.range p.T,
                                      AnvBandit.dEll (↑p.H) p.β (j + 1) *

                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (M.supOthers c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                              (∑ j ∈ Finset.range p.T,
                                AnvBandit.dEll (↑p.H) p.β (j + 1) *
                                  (AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedPotential M p C lam j -
                                    AnvBandit.Absorbing.expectedPotential M p C lam (j + 1)))
                              (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                                  (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))))
                          (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (M.supOthers c) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                                (AnvBandit.M1
                                  (have this := p.hn;
                                  Decidable.byContradiction fun a =>
                                    AnvBandit.Absorbing.missing_drift_budget._proof_1 this a)
                                  C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star))

                                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero
                                  (M.Flam (AnvBandit.Absorbing.unitCost M) p.L lam) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 2))
                                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 2))
                                      (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))))
                            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0))))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_le_of_neg
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_nonpos (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le h0)
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le h1))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_neg_of_lt a))))))
    (have h := AnvBandit.Absorbing.potential_series_bound M p C lam p.T;
    Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrFun'
          (congrArg LE.le
            (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl (Finset.range p.T)) fun x a =>

            (AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p))))

theorem AnvBandit.concreteKbeta_summable : ∀ {n : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (i0 : Fin n)
  {H β : ℝ},
  0 ≤ H →
    1 < β → ∀ (r : ℕ), Summable fun ℓ => AnvBandit.scheduleOmega H β (ℓ + 1) * AnvBandit.remainingV ⋯ C i0 (ℓ - r) :=
fun {n} hn C i0 {H β} hH hβ r =>
  have hd :=
    have this := cast (Eq.symm Nat.cast_le._simp_1) hn;
    lt_of_not_ge fun a =>
      Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.lt_irrefl
        (Eq.mp
          (congrArg (fun _a => _a < 0)
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.of_eq
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_congr
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))
                      Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 2)))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (↑n) rfl
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg (fun _a => ↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                        (Eq.refl (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_mul (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                              (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                              (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1)))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_lt (Nat.rawCast 2)
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add
                          (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap
                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_raw_eq
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 2))
                          (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 1)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + 0))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_congr
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.atom_pf (↑n) rfl
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg (fun _a => ↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 = ↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * _a)
                            (Eq.symm rfl)))
                        (Eq.refl (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_pos (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_one))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_add
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_raw_eq
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_neg (Eq.refl Neg.neg)
                            (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                            (Eq.refl (Int.negOfNat 1))))
                        Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero)
                      (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_gt (Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast
                        (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.sub_pf Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.neg_zero
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_zero
                      ((Int.negOfNat 1).rawCast + (↑n ^ Nat.rawCast 1 * Nat.rawCast 1 + 0)))))
                (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                  (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                    (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1)) (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0))))
                  (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_add_overlap_zero
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_overlap_pf_zero (↑n) (Nat.rawCast 1)
                      (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.to_isNat
                        (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isInt_add (Eq.refl HAdd.hAdd)
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsInt.of_raw ℝ (Int.negOfNat 1))
                          (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.to_isInt (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.IsNat.of_raw ℝ 1))
                          (Eq.refl (Int.ofNat 0)))))
                    (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.Common.add_pf_zero_add 0))))
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Ring.cast_zero (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ Nat.cast_zero))))
          (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_neg_of_le
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.add_lt_of_neg_of_le (neg_neg_of_pos Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.zero_lt_one)
              (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le this))
            (Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith.sub_nonpos_of_le a)));
  AnvBandit.Kbeta_summable
    (mul_nonneg
      (le_of_lt
        (Mathlib.Meta.Positivity.pos_of_isNat (Mathlib.Meta.NormNum.isNat_ofNat ℝ (Eq.refl 2)) (Eq.refl (Nat.ble 1 2))))
      hH)
    (mul_nonneg (id (Finset.sum_nonneg fun x x_1 => ENNReal.toReal_nonneg)) (LT.lt.le (Real.exp_pos 1)))
    (AnvBandit.scheduleOmega_nonneg H β)
    (AnvBandit.remainingV_nonneg (Decidable.byContradiction fun a => AnvBandit.remainingV_geometric._proof_1 hn a) C i0)
    (Real.exp_pos (-(AnvBandit.epsP C / (↑n - 1))))
    (Real.exp_lt_one_iff.mpr (neg_neg_of_pos (div_pos (AnvBandit.epsP_pos C) hd))) ⌈β⌉₊
    (AnvBandit.scheduleOmega_le_polynomial hH hβ) (AnvBandit.remainingV_geometric hn C i0) r
'AnvBandit.concreteKbeta_summable' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
